# Results by test case

For readablility some shortcuts are applied:

- `named`
  - `named: 'named'`
- `[named]`
  - `[named]: 'named'` (non enumerable)
- `default`
  - `default: 'default'`
- `[default]`
  - `[default]: 'default'` (non enumerable)
- `__esModule`
  - `__esModule: true`
- `[__esModule]`
  - `[__esModule]: true` (non enumerable)
- `[G]`
  - `[Getter]`
- `[Module]`

  - `[Symbol(Symbol.toStringTag)]: 'Module'`

- 💎 hard outlier
- 🟡 outlier
- ✅ very common value

<!-- module results -->
